<span class="ct-node-label">AI Doom</span>
<span class="ct-node-title-full">Could Advanced AI Permanently Escape Human Control?</span>
<span class="ct-node-summary">“AI doom” is shorthand for the possibility that advanced artificial intelligence could cause human extinction, permanent civilisational collapse, or an irreversible loss of humanity’s control over its future. The central concern is not that today’s chatbots will suddenly become murderous.</span>
</span>
